before reaching a deep drawer or cavity target, but the predicate can accept an over-edge drop into the container.\\n- [Order multi-place tasks so nothing is carried or re-grasped over an already-placed fragile object](global/place-order-never-carry-over-placed-object.md) \\u2014 a task requires placing two or more objects and one placement (e.g. an upright mug on a plate) is easily tipped by a later carry\\n- [Switch to point prompts when text masks ignore the intended relation.](global/point-prompt-after-text-misground.md) \\u2014 A scene has duplicate objects or look-alike circular surfaces and SAM text segmentation selects the wrong instance\\n- [Rotate a held bowl after a reliable grasp to move its offset into reach](global/post-grasp-yaw-offset.md) \\u2014 A reliable Pi0 bowl grasp carries the object with a large offset that makes direct placement miss or hit the workspace edge\\n- [After releasing an object into a container, retreat the gripper straight up before judging the predicate](global/predicate-fires-after-gripper-retreat.md) \\u2014 you released a held object into/onto a target and libero_terminated is still false\\n- [Retreat the eef fully clear of a placement before concluding the predicate failed](global/predicate-gated-by-eef-proximity-retreat-clear.md) \\u2014 after a geometrically-correct place, libero_terminated stays false for many steps while the gripper hovers over the just-placed object\\n- [Recenter after pre-grasp yaw before trusting Pi0.](global/pregrasp-yaw-recenter.md) \\u2014 A pre-grasp wrist yaw is used to change a bowl or cup rim-hook geometry, and the rotation shifts the wrist or EEF away from the visually selected target\\n- [Find a container's true floor by descend-until-OSC-stall and read the stall by rim/liner/other objects) and depth/segment centroids are rim-biased\\n- [Anchor duplicate-object wrist refinement to a visible relation landmark.](global/relation-anchored-wrist-refine.md) \\u2014 A task chooses one of multiple identical objects by a spatial relation, and wrist views can contain the wrong duplicate.\\n- [Select identical targets by the named spatial relation before wrist refinement](global/relation-selected-identical-object.md) \\u2014 A task contains duplicate or visually identical objects and names a relation such as on, left of, right of, between, or next to\\n- [Remeasure a held payload after every loaded servo before descending](global/remeasure-loaded-offset-before-descent.md) \\u2014 a large asymmetric object is carried by a handle and precise support placement depends on body-to-EEF offset\\n- [After releasing into a lined basket, retreat straight up before moving laterally](global/retreat-high-before-lateral-near-lined-basket.md) \\u2014 placing items into a woven/cloth-lined basket or any container with a soft rim/liner that stands above the rigid rim; especially multi-item drop-ins where you must traverse away between placements\\n- [Clear a released wrist by reversing the corridor that successfully entered.](global/reverse-entry-corridor-clearance.md) \\u2014 A released object is cavity-side but axial or vertical wrist retreat re-hooks central visual bands fail despite clean object placement\\n- [Use a capped contact skill to seat rim-perched objects into containers.](global/rim-perch-contact-seat.md) \\u2014 An object has been look-alike cans by RGB label, never by SAM3 brand-noun prompt](global/sam3-brand-noun-can-collision.md) \\u2014 two or more similar cans/cylinders are present and the task names one by brand (\\\"tomato sauce\\\", \\\"alphabet soup\\\", a soup can)\\n- [Re-issue a pick with a nearby landmark when generic picking makes only contact.](global/spatial-qualified-repick.md) \\u2014 A generic Can top back-projects around z=0.06-0.08 on this frame.\n\nControlled basket descent: `step_clip=0.004-0.006`, target z about 0.155-0.17, but expect OSC/contact to stall near eef z\u22480.195 when the can/rim contacts.\n\n`pi0_doubled max_chunks=12` (band 10-14) from the rim-perched state was enough; stop when termination fires.\n\nNEVER release a cylindrical can high over the basket from eef z\u22480.21 unless the can is fully over the cavity; A1 bounced it out.\n\nNEVER assume basket region medians are the cavity center; the white liner/rim and table pixels bias the estimate.\n\n## Failure modes\n| symptom | root cause (A<N>) | fix |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| Correct can bounces out after release | A1 released a cylindrical can high at eef z\u22480.21 near the front/right basket lip | Bias placement deeper/backer and avoid high drop for cans |\n| Lower release still leaves can perched | A1 and A2 scripted lowering/pushing contacted the rim/can but did not create the final seating motion | From the perched state run capped `pi0_doubled` with an explicit push-into-basket prompt |\n| Pi0 reports pick failure although object is held | A2 gripper threshold did not classify the grasp as success, but wrist/agentview showed the tomato can in the fingers | Use image + gripper state as the grasp judge, not the Pi0 success flag |\n\n## Re-localization per scene\nTomato sauce: in agentview_high, choose the red/green cylindrical can with tomato graphics. It can be confused with the orange ketchup bottle by the word tomato and with the blue/yellow alphabet soup can by shape. Prefer manual pixels on the metal lid/upper colored band; reject lower side pixels that back-project to table z. Wrist refinement is accepted only if it stays within about 3-5 cm of the agentview identity anchor.\n\nBasket: visually identify the white-lined woven square container. Use agentview for identity and wrist for geometry when close. The true useful target is the open liner/cavity, not the woven rim or outside wall. In this solved run, absolute basket/can coordinates are counter-examples only and must not be cached.\n\n## Fragility flags\nMost fragile step is post-release seating. If the can is perched and not terminated, do not grind many long scripted pushes; re-close, make at most a short controlled inward/downward contact, then use `pi0_doubled` with a direct seating prompt.\n\nSecond fragility is Pi0 over-executing the pick into a partial place. If it carries the correct can over the basket, salvage by firming the grip and lowering/releasing; do not reset just because the pick skill moved farther than expected.\n\n## Difficulty and reliability\nSolved on attempt 2 after one failed high-release/perch episode. Expected single-shot reliability is moderate if the contact-skill fallback is used immediately after a rim perch; lower if relying only on scripted OSC pushes. Remaining uncertainty: whether a pure low release can terminate without `pi0_doubled` from other seeds was not established.\n\n## Cross-refs\n[[brand-label-over-segmentation]]\n[[rim-perch-contact-seat]]\n"
